# Third-party licenses

Capybara's D2 Editor is MIT licensed (see [LICENSE](LICENSE)). Its release exe, built with `cargo build --release -p capy-app` for `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`, statically links the third-party Rust packages listed here and embeds the data files and fonts described in section 3. This file reproduces their licenses and notices, as those licenses require.

This file is generated by `python -I scripts/third_party_licenses.py` from `Cargo.lock`. Do not edit it by hand: re-run the script after any dependency change (`--check` exits 1 when this file is stale). For credits and the history of the file formats, see [NOTICE](NOTICE).

- Third-party packages in the exe: **374**
- First-party workspace crates in the exe (MIT, not listed): capy-app, capy-cli, capy-edit, capy-history, capy-index, capy-paint, capy-render, capy-sprite, d2-assets, d2-formats
- Build-time only, not shipped: 62 (section 7)
- Flagged for review: 19 (section 2)

Contents:

## 2. Flagged packages

Allowed without review: 0BSD, Apache-2.0, BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause, BSL-1.0, CC0-1.0, ISC, MIT, MIT-0, NCSA, Unicode-3.0, Unicode-DFS-2016, Unlicense, Zlib, bzip2-1.0.6, and `Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception`. For an `OR` expression, the most permissive branch is elected. Everything else is listed here: copyleft (GPL, LGPL, AGPL and the like), unknown or missing licenses, licenses with conditions beyond attribution, and crates that ship no license text.

No package requires a copyleft license: **yes**.

What the conditions require:

- **MPL-2.0**: MPL-2.0 is file-level (weak) copyleft. The exe may stay MIT, but the MPL-covered source files must remain under MPL-2.0: anyone who gets the exe must be told how to get the source of those files (a link to the crate's published source is enough), including any changes we make to them. Unmodified use from crates.io needs only this notice and the link.
- **OFL-1.1**: SIL Open Font License 1.1. Embedding the font in a program is allowed. The copyright notice and the license must accompany it, the font may not be sold on its own, and a modified font may not use the Reserved Font Names.
- **Ubuntu-font-1.0**: Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0. Embedding the font is allowed. The copyright notice and the licence must accompany it, and a modified font must be renamed and stay under the same licence.

Dual-licensed packages that also offer a copyleft or conditional license; the permissive branch is elected:

- `self_cell 1.3.0`: `Apache-2.0 OR GPL-2.0-only` (not elected: GPL-2.0-only; the text of the license not elected is left out of section 5)

## 3. Data, fonts and native code

These are compiled into the exe but are not cargo packages, or are inside a package but carry their own license.

### Program icon (`assets/branding/capybara.ico`)

License: project's own artwork, same license as the program. Embedded by: capy-app.

The capybara icon of the author's earlier MXL Capybara Editor, used as the exe, window and loading-screen icon. It is the project's own artwork and is covered by this program's license.

### DS1 object table (`assets/editor/obj.txt`)

License: MIT (DS1Edit), over an unlicensed original by Paul Siramy. Embedded by: d2-assets.

It maps DS1 object and monster ids to the COF token, mode and class the preview needs. It comes from DS1Edit by Ben Ethington (https://github.com/bethington/d2-ds1-edit), which is MIT licensed; its license is reproduced below.

DS1Edit's own NOTICE says this table is Paul Siramy's, from win_ds1edit (http://paul.siramy.free.fr/_divers/ds1/dl_ds1edit.html), and that DS1Edit's MIT license covers only DS1Edit's changes and is not a relicensing of Paul's work. win_ds1edit was published with its source from 2002 on, with no license text and no copyright notice. The table is used here on the same basis as DS1Edit uses it: with attribution to Paul Siramy.

### Known-names seed (`assets/known-names/seed.txt`)

License: list of game file names; sources credited. Embedded by: d2-assets.

A list of about 200 Diablo II game file paths that the game or the editor loads by hard-coded name. It holds names only, no game data. The names were collected from DS1Edit's format guides and source string literals (MIT, license above) and from common Diablo II conventions; see `assets/known-names/README.md`. The files they name belong to Blizzard Entertainment and are not distributed with this program.

### Fonts embedded by egui (`epaint_default_fonts 0.36.2`)

`bevy_egui`'s `default_fonts` feature turns on egui's built-in fonts. The `epaint_default_fonts` crate (`(MIT OR Apache-2.0) AND OFL-1.1 AND Ubuntu-font-1.0`) compiles these font files into the exe with `include_bytes!`. Copyright and version below are read from each font's own `name` table. Bevy's text stack (`bevy_text`) is not enabled, so Bevy embeds no font.

| Font file | Use | License | Copyright (from the font) | Version | License file |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Hack-Regular.ttf` | Hack (Source Foundry), monospace text | `MIT AND Bitstream-Vera` | Copyright (c) 2018 Source Foundry Authors / Copyright (c) 2003 by Bitstream, Inc. All Rights Reserved. | Version 3.003 | `fonts/Hack-Regular.txt` |
| `NotoEmoji-Regular.ttf` | Noto Emoji (Google), emoji glyphs | `OFL-1.1` | Copyright 2013 Google Inc. All Rights Reserved. | Version 1.05 uh | `fonts/OFL.txt` |
| `Ubuntu-Light.ttf` | Ubuntu Light (Canonical), proportional text | `Ubuntu-font-1.0` | Copyright 2011 Canonical Ltd. Licensed under the Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0 | 0.83 | `fonts/UFL.txt` |
| `emoji-icon-font.ttf` | emoji-icon-font (John Slegers), icon glyphs | `MIT` | (none in font) | Version 1.1 | `fonts/emoji-icon-font-mit-license.txt` |

### Data embedded by Rust packages

- `jiff-tzdb 0.1.9` embeds the IANA Time Zone Database (https://www.iana.org/time-zones), which is in the public domain. The crate's code is `Unlicense OR MIT`.

### Native code compiled into the exe

| Package | Build tools | What is compiled |
|---|---|---|
| `blake3 1.8.7` | cc | `pure` feature: no C or assembly is compiled; Rust only. |
| `libsqlite3-sys 0.38.2` | cc | SQLite, compiled from the amalgamation bundled in the crate (`bundled` feature). SQLite is in the public domain. |

SQLite 3.53.2 (https://sqlite.org/), bundled by `libsqlite3-sys`, carries this dedication instead of a license:

```text
The author disclaims copyright to this source code.  In place of
a legal notice, here is a blessing:

   May you do good and not evil.
   May you find forgiveness for yourself and forgive others.
   May you share freely, never taking more than you give.
```

## 5. License texts

Each distinct license text appears once (179 texts), followed by the packages that ship it and the file name in each package. Texts that differ only in whitespace are merged. Dual-licensed packages appear under each of their texts.

### Text 1: Apache-2.0

### Text 173: Apache-2.0 + BSD + BSL-1.0

Used by: `lexical 7.0.5` (LICENSE.md), `lexical-core 1.0.6` (LICENSE.md), `lexical-parse-float 1.0.6` (LICENSE.md), `lexical-parse-integer 1.0.6` (LICENSE.md), `lexical-write-float 1.0.6` (LICENSE.md), `lexical-write-integer 1.0.6` (LICENSE.md)

```text
# Licensing

Lexical is dual licensed under the Apache 2.0 license as well as the MIT
license. See the LICENCE-MIT and the LICENCE-APACHE files for the licenses.

Other licensing terms may apply, as described in depth below for various features and functionality. All assume use of `lexical` or `lexical-core`.

## `write-floats, not(compact)`

`lexical-write-float/src/algorithm.rs` is a direct port of the reference C++ implementation of Dragonbox, found [here](https://github.com/jk-jeon/dragonbox/).
This code (used if the `write-floats` feature is enabled and the `compact` feature is disabled) is subject to a [Boost Software License](https://github.com/jk-jeon/dragonbox/blob/71993f55067a89f4b4e27591605e21521f5c61be/LICENSE-Boost) and a modified [Apache2 license](https://github.com/jk-jeon/dragonbox/blob/71993f55067a89f4b4e27591605e21521f5c61be/LICENSE-Apache2-LLVM), shown in the [Boost Software License](#boost-software-license) and [Apache2 With LLVM Exceptions](#apache2-with-llvm-exceptions) sections below.

## `write-floats, compact`

`lexical-write-float/src/compact.rs` is a direct port of a C++ implementation of the Grisu algorithm, found [here](https://github.com/night-shift/fpconv/).
This code (used if both the `write-floats` and `compact` features are enabled) is subject to a [MIT License](https://github.com/night-shift/fpconv/blob/dfeb7e938fb85fb5eca130b84f856705ced75012/license), shown in the [fpconv License](#fpconv-license) section below.

## `write-floats, radix`

`lexical-write-float/src/radix.rs` is adapted from the V8 implementation found [here](). This code (used if both the `parse-floats` and `radix` features are enabled) is subject to a [3-clause BSD license](https://github.com/v8/v8/blob/f80bfeaf0792652bfbc1f174d5a7b8ab8bc0cbbd/LICENSE.v8), shown in the [V8 License](#v8-license) section below.

## `parse-floats, compact`

`lexical-parse-float/src/bellerophon.rs` is loosely based off the Golang implementation,
found [here](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/b10849fbb97a2244c086991b4623ae9f32c212d0/src/strconv/extfloat.go). This code (used if both the `parse-floats` and `compact` features are enabled) is subject to a [3-clause BSD license](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/b10849fbb97a2244c086991b4623ae9f32c212d0/LICENSE), shown in the [Go License](#go-license) section below.

# License Terms

This contains complete copies of the licensing terms for the feature-dependent code described above.
```
